Given:

\n\n

(2 cos3 A - cos A) = (sin A - 2 sin3 A)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

(2 cos3 A - cos A) = (sin A - 2 sin3 A)

\n\n

⇒ (2 cos3 A + 2 sin3 A) = (sin A + cos A)

\n\n

⇒ 2(cos A + sin A)(cos2 A + sin2 A - sin A.cos A) = (cos A + sin A)

\n\n

⇒ 2(1 - sin A.cos A) = 1

\n\n

⇒ 2sin A.cos A = 1

\n\n

⇒ sin 2A = 1 = sin 90°

\n\n

⇒ 2A = 90°

\n\n

⇒ A = 45°

\n\n

Hence, the value of A is 45°.

", "hn": "\n

(2 cos3 A - cos A)/(sin A - 2 sin3 A) = 1

\n\n

⇒ (2 cos3 A - cos A) = (sin A - 2 sin3 A)

\n\n

⇒ (2 cos3 A + 2 sin3 A) = (sin A + cos A)

\n\n

⇒ 2(cos A + sin A)(cos2 A + sin2 A - sin A.cos A) = (cos A + sin A)

\n\n

⇒ 2(1 - sin A.cos A) = 1

\n\n

⇒ 2sin A.cos A = 1

\n\n

⇒ sin 2A = 1 = sin 90°

\n\n

⇒ 2A = 90°

\n\n

⇒ A = 45°

\n\n

अत:, A का मान 45° है।

⇒ A = 45°

\n\n

તેથી, A નું મૂલ્ય 45° છે.

\n\n94:T4a42,\n

ಇದೆಲ್ಲವನ್ನೂ ನೀಡಲಾಗಿದೆ:

\n\n

(2 cos3 A - cos A) = (sin A - 2 sin3 A)

\n\n

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

\n\n

(2 cos3 A - cos A) = (sin A - 2 sin3 A)

\n\n

⇒ (2 cos3 A + 2 sin3 A) = (sin A + cos A)

\n\n

⇒ 2(cos A + sin A)(cos2 A + sin2 A - sin A.cos A) = (cos A + sin A)

\n\n

⇒ 2(1 - sin A.cos A) = 1

\n\n

⇒ 2sin A.cos A = 1

\n\n

⇒ sin 2A = 1 = sin 90°

\n\n

⇒ 2A = 90°

\n\n

⇒ A = 45°

\n\n

ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, A ನ ಮೌಲ್ಯ 45° ಆಗಿದೆ.

Shortcut Trick\n

Simplify the expression directly using basic trigonometric identities:

\n

We know that (1 + cot2θ) = cosec2θ and (cosec2θ − cot2θ) = 1

\n

Given expression = (secθ × cosec2θ × 1) ÷ cosec3θ

\n

⇒ secθ ÷ cosecθ = (1 / cosθ) ÷ (1 / sinθ) = sinθ / cosθ = tanθ

\n

Since cotθ = 4/3, we get tanθ = 1 / cotθ = 3/4.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3/4.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

cotθ = 4/3 = Base / Perpendicular

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Hypotenuse = √(Base2 + Perpendicular2) = √(42 + 32) = √25 = 5

\n

⇒ secθ = Hypotenuse / Base = 5/4

\n

⇒ cosecθ = Hypotenuse / Perpendicular = 5/3

\n

Substitute these values into the given expression:

\n

⇒ Numerator = secθ × (1 + cot2θ) × (cosec2θ − cot2θ)

\n

⇒ Numerator = (5/4) × (1 + 16/9) × (25/9 − 16/9)

\n

⇒ Numerator = (5/4) × (25/9) × (9/9) = 125/36

\n

⇒ Denominator = cosec3θ = (5/3)3 = 125/27

\n

⇒ Expression value = (125/36) ÷ (125/27) = (125/36) × (27/125)

\n

⇒ Expression value = 27/36 = 3/4

\n

∴ The correct answer is 3/4.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Fundamental Trigonometric Identities

\n

The three primary square identities are: sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, sec2θ − tan2θ = 1, and cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1.

\n

Reciprocal Relations

\n

The reciprocal of sine is cosecant (cosecθ = 1/sinθ), cosine is secant (secθ = 1/cosθ), and tangent is cotangent (cotθ = 1/tanθ).

\n

Pythagoras Theorem

\n

In any right-angled triangle, the square of the hypotenuse is always equal to the sum of the squares of the other two sides: Hypotenuse2 = Base2 + Perpendicular2.

Shortcut Trick\n

ప్రాథమిక త్రికోణమితి సర్వసమీకరణాలను ఉపయోగించి ఇచ్చిన సమాసాన్ని నేరుగా సరళీకరించండి:

"solution_html": {"en": "\n

Given:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{sin\\, θ-cos\\, θ}{sin\\, θ+cos\\, θ}=\\frac{4}{5}\\)

\n\n

Concept used:

\n\n

By componendo & dividendo rule:

\n\n

⇒ (a + b)/(a - b) = p/q

\n\n

⇒ a/b = (p + q)/(p - q)

\n\n

By Pythagorean theorem:

\n\n

H2 = P2 + B2

\n\n

Where P = perpendicular ; B = base

\n\n

and H = hypotenuse

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

(sin θ + cos θ)/(sin θ - cos θ) = 5/4

\n\n

By componendo & dividendo rule:

\n\n

⇒ Sin θ/cos θ = (5 + 4)/(5 - 4)

\n\n

⇒ Tan θ = 9

\n\n

Here, P = 9 & B = 1

\n\n

By Pythagorean theorem:

\n\n

H2 = P2 + B2

\n\n

⇒ H =  √(92  + 1) 

\n\n

⇒ H = √82

\n\n

\\(\\frac{cosec^2\\theta}{2-cosec^2\\theta}\\)

\n\n

⇒ (√82/9)2/{2 - (√82/9)2}

\n\n

⇒ (82/81)/{2 - 82/81}

\n\n

⇒ (82/81)/{(162 - 82)/81}

\n\n

⇒ (82/81)/{(80/81}

\n\n

⇒ 82/80 = \\(\\frac{41}{40}\\)

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is \\(\\frac{41}{40}\\).

\n\n

\n\n

ನೀಡಲಾಗಿದೆ:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{sin\\, θ-cos\\, θ}{sin\\, θ+cos\\, θ}=\\frac{4}{5}\\)

\n\n

ಬಳಸಿದ ಪರಿಕಲ್ಪನೆ:

\n\n

ಕಂಪೋನೆಂಡೋ ಮತ್ತು ಡಿವಿಡೆಂಡೋ ನಿಯಮದಿಂದ:

\n\n

⇒ (a + b)/(a - b) = p/q

\n\n

⇒ a/b = (p + q)/(p - q)

\n\n

ಪೈಥಾಗರಿಯನ್ ಪ್ರಮೇಯದಿಂದ:

\n\n

H2 = P2 + B2

\n\n

ಇಲ್ಲಿ P = ಲಂಬ; B = ಆಧಾರ

\n\n

ಮತ್ತು H = ಕರ್ಣ

\n\n

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

\n\n

(sin θ + cos θ)/(sin θ - cos θ) = 5/4

\n\n

ಕಂಪೋನೆಂಡೋ ಮತ್ತು ಡಿವಿಡೆಂಡೋ ನಿಯಮದಿಂದ:

\n\n

⇒ Sin θ/cos θ = (5 + 4)/(5 - 4)

\n\n

⇒ Tan θ = 9

\n\n

ಇಲ್ಲಿ, P = 9 ಮತ್ತು B = 1

\n\n

ಪೈಥಾಗರಿಯನ್ ಪ್ರಮೇಯದಿಂದ:

\n\n

H2 = P2 + B2

\n\n

⇒ H = √(92 + 1)

\n\n

⇒ H = √82

\n\n

\\(\\frac{cosec^2\\theta}{2-cosec^2\\theta}\\)

\n\n

⇒ (√82/9)2/{2 - (√82/9)2}

\n\n

⇒ (82/81)/{2 - 82/81}

\n\n

⇒ (82/81)/{(162 - 82)/81}

\n\n

⇒ (82/81)/{(80/81}

\n\n

⇒ 82/80 = \\(\\frac{41}{40}\\)

\n\n

∴ ಸರಿಯಾದ ಉತ್ತರ \\(\\frac{41}{40}\\).

ನೀಡಲಾಗಿದೆ​:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{\\sin A}{\\cot A+\\operatorname{cosec} A}-\\frac{\\sin A}{\\cot A-\\operatorname{cosec} A}-1\\)

\n\n

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

\n\n

⇒ ಛೇದಗಳ LCM ಅನ್ನು ತೆಗೆದುಕೊಂಡರೆ, ನಾವು ಪಡೆಯುತ್ತೇವೆ

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{sinA(cot A -cosec A)-sin A(cot A+cosec A)-(cot A+cosecA)(cotA-cosec A)}{(cotA+cosec A)(cotA-cosecA)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{sinAcotA-sinAcosecA-sinAcotA-sinAcosecA-cot^2A+cosec^2A}{cot^2A-cosec^2A}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{-2sinAcosecA-cot^2A+cosec^2A}{-(cosec^2A-cot^2A)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ ಗುರುತನ್ನು ಬಳಸಿ, 1 + cot2A = cosec2A, ನಾವು ಪಡೆಯುತ್ತೇವೆ

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{-2sinAcosecA-cot^2A+cosec^2A}{-1}\\)

\n\n

⇒ cot2A – cosec2A + 2sinA cosecA

\n\n

⇒ 2sinA × \\(\\frac{1}{sinA}\\) – 1 = 2 – 1 = 1

\n\n

ಆದ್ದರಿಂದ, ಸಮೀಕರಣದ ಅಗತ್ಯವಿರುವ ಮೌಲ್ಯವು 1 ಆಗಿದೆ.

गणना:

\n\n

 5sin Shortcut Trick\n

If the sum of two angles is 90°, then sin θ1 = cos θ2.

\n

Check the sum of the given angles: (50° + A) + (40° − A) = 90°.

\n

Thus, sin(50° + A) = cos(40° − A).

\n

Subtracting them directly gives: sin(50° + A) − cos(40° − A) = 0.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 0.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Expression: sin(50° + A) − cos(40° − A)

\n

Formula Used:

\n

sin θ = cos(90° − θ)

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let θ = 50° + A

\n

⇒ Using the complementary angle formula: sin θ = cos(90° − θ)

\n

⇒ sin(50° + A) = cos(90° − (50° + A))

\n

⇒ sin(50° + A) = cos(90° − 50° − A)

\n

⇒ sin(50° + A) = cos(40° − A)

\n

⇒ Substitute this result into the original expression:

\n

⇒ cos(40° − A) − cos(40° − A) = 0

\n

∴ The correct answer is 0.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Complementary Angles

\n

Two angles are complementary if their sum is 90°. Key formulas: sin(90° − θ) = cos θ, sec(90° − θ) = cosec θ, and tan(90° − θ) = cot θ.

\n

Supplementary Angles

\n

Two angles are supplementary if their sum is 180°. Key formulas: sin(180° − θ) = sin θ, and cos(180° − θ) = −cos θ.

\n

Basic Trigonometric Identities

\n

1) sin2θ + cos2θ = 1
2) sec2θ − tan2θ = 1
3) cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1

Shortcut Trick

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} θ\\left[\\frac{1+\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}+\\frac{\\sin θ}{1+\\cos θ}\\right]-2 \\cot ^2 θ\\)

\n\n

Put θ = 90° 

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} 90\\left[\\frac{1+\\cos 90}{\\sin 90}+\\frac{\\sin 90}{1+\\cos 90}\\right]-2 \\cot ^2 90\\)

\n\n

⇒ 1 [\\({1+0}\\over1\\) + \\({1}\\over{1+0}\\)] - 2 (0)2

\n\n

⇒ 1 [ 1 + 1] - 2 × 0

\n\n

⇒ 1 × 2 = 2

\n\n

Hence, the correct answer is option (3).

\n\n

Alternate Method 

\n\n

Solution:

\n\n

We are given the expression:

\n\n

\\(\\csc θ \\left[ \\frac{1 + \\cos θ}{\\sin θ} + \\frac{\\sin θ}{1 + \\cos θ} \\right] - 2 \\cot^2 θ\\)

\n\n

Step 1: Simplify the term inside the brackets

\n\n

We have:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{1 + \\cos θ}{\\sin θ} + \\frac{\\sin θ}{1 + \\cos θ} = \\frac{(1 + \\cos θ)^2 + \\sin^2 θ}{\\sin θ (1 + \\cos θ)} = \\frac{2(1 + \\cos θ)}{\\sin θ (1 + \\cos θ)}\\)

\n\n

This simplifies to 2 / sinθ .

\n\n

Step 2: Multiply by cscθcscθcsc⁡θ

\n\n

We multiply by csc θ = 1 / sinθ , so we get 2 / sinθ.

\n\n

Step 3: Handle the second term

\n\n

We have \\(2 \\cot^2 θ = 2 \\frac{\\cos^2 θ}{\\sin^2 θ}.\\)

\n\n

Step 4: Combine both terms

\n\n

The expression becomes:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{2}{\\sin^2 θ} - 2 \\frac{\\cos^2 θ}{\\sin^2 θ} = \\frac{2}{\\sin^2 θ} × (1 - \\cos^2 θ).\\)

\n\n

Since \\( 1 - \\cos^2 θ = \\sin^2 θ\\), the expression simplifies to 2.

\n\n

∴ The value of the expression is 2.

Shortcut Trick 

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} θ\\left[\\frac{1+\\cos θ}{\\sin θ}+\\frac{\\sin θ}{1+\\cos θ}\\right]-2 \\cot ^2 θ\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\\(\\operatorname{cosec} 90\\left[\\frac{1+\\cos 90}{\\sin 90}+\\frac{\\sin 90}{1+\\cos 90}\\right]-2 \\cot ^2 90\\)

\n\n

⇒ 1 [ \\({1+0}\\over1\\) + \\({1}\\over{1+0}\\) ] - 2 (0) 2

\n\n

⇒ 1 [ 1 + 1 ] - 2 × 0

\n\n

⇒ 1 × 2 = 2

\n\n

अतः सही उत्तर विकल्प (3) है।

\n\n

वैकल्पिक विधि  

\n\n

समाधान:

\n\n

हमें अभिव्यक्ति दी गई है:

\n\n

\\(\\csc θ \\left[ \\frac{1 + \\cos θ}{\\sin θ} + \\frac{\\sin θ}{1 + \\cos θ} \\right] - 2 \\cot^2 θ\\)

\n\n

चरण 1: कोष्ठक के अंदर दिए गए पद को सरल करें

\n\n

हमारे पास है:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{1 + \\cos θ}{\\sin θ} + \\frac{\\sin θ}{1 + \\cos θ} = \\frac{(1 + \\cos θ)^2 + \\sin^2 θ}{\\sin θ (1 + \\cos θ)} = \\frac{2(1 + \\cos θ)}{\\sin θ (1 + \\cos θ)}\\)

\n\n

यह सरल होकर 2 / sinθ हो जाता है।

\n\n

चरण 2: से गुणा करें सीएससीθ सीएससी θ csc ⁡ θ

\n\n

हम csc θ = 1 / sinθ से गुणा करते हैं, तो हमें 2 / sinθ प्राप्त होता है।

\n\n

चरण 3: दूसरे कार्यकाल को संभालें

\n\n

हमारे पास \\(2 \\cot^2 θ = 2 \\frac{\\cos^2 θ}{\\sin^2 θ}.\\) है

\n\n

चरण 4: दोनों पदों को मिलाएं

\n\n

अभिव्यक्ति इस प्रकार है:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{2}{\\sin^2 θ} - 2 \\frac{\\cos^2 θ}{\\sin^2 θ} = \\frac{2}{\\sin^2 θ} × (1 - \\cos^2 θ).\\)

\n\n

चूँकि \\( 1 - \\cos^2 θ = \\sin^2 θ\\) , व्यंजक सरल होकर 2 हो जाता है।

\n\n

∴ व्यंजक का मान 2 है।

\n\\(\\frac{x^2}{p^2} + \\frac{y^2}{q^2} - \\frac{z^2}{r^2}\\)", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "1"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "\\(p^2 + q^2 - r^2 \\)"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "\\(​p^2 - q^2 + r^2\\)"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "0"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Put standard angles A = 0° and B = 0° to quickly solve the expression.

\n

⇒ x = p(1)(1) = p, y = q(1)(0) = 0, and z = r(0) = 0

\n

Substitute these calculated values into the expression (x2 / p2) + (y2 / q2) − (z2 / r2):

\n

⇒ (p2 / p2) + 0 − 0 = 1

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

⇒ x = p sec A cos B

\n

⇒ y = q sec A sin B

\n

⇒ z = r tan A

\n

Formula Used:

\n

⇒ sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1

\n

⇒ sec2 θ − tan2 θ = 1

\n

Calculations:

\n

Rearranging the given equations, we get:

\n

⇒ x / p = sec A cos B

\n

⇒ y / q = sec A sin B

\n

⇒ z / r = tan A

\n

Substitute these values into the required expression:

\n

⇒ (x2 / p2) + (y2 / q2) − (z2 / r2)

\n

⇒ (sec A cos B)2 + (sec A sin B)2 − (tan A)2

\n

⇒ sec2 A cos2 B + sec2 A sin2 B − tan2 A

\n

Taking sec2 A common from the first two terms:

\n

⇒ sec2 A (cos2 B + sin2 B) − tan2 A

\n

Using the identity cos2 B + sin2 B = 1:

\n

⇒ sec2 A (1) − tan2 A

\n

⇒ sec2 A − tan2 A

\n

Using the identity sec2 A − tan2 A = 1:

\n

⇒ 1

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Pythagorean Identities

\n

These fundamental relations form the basis of trigonometric simplifications: sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1, sec2 θ − tan2 θ = 1, and cosec2 θ − cot2 θ = 1.

\n

Value Substitution Method

\n

For pure variable trigonometric expressions, substituting standard angles (like 0°, 30°, 45°, 60°, 90°) provides the fastest route to the solution by avoiding complex expansions.

\n

Trigonometric Ratios at 0°

\n

At an angle of 0°, sin 0° = 0, cos 0° = 1, tan 0° = 0, and sec 0° = 1. These specific values are extremely helpful in eliminating terms rapidly from algebraic-trigonometric equations.

கொடுக்கப்பட்டது:

2 sinθ + 2 sin 2 θ = 2

பயன்படுத்தப்பட்ட கருத்து:

பாவம் 2 θ + காஸ் 2 θ = 1

கணக்கீடு:

2 sinθ + 2 sin 2 θ = 2

⇒ sinθ + sin 2 θ = 1

⇒ sin θ + 1 - cos 2 θ = 1

⇒ பாவம் θ = காஸ் 2 θ

⇒ cos 4 θ = sin 2 θ

அதனால்,

2 cos 4 θ + 2 cos 2 θ = 2 sin 2 θ + 2 sin θ

⇒ 2

∴ தேவையான பதில் 2.

\n\\(\\sqrt{\\frac{\\sec \\theta + 1}{\\sec \\theta - 1}} + \\sqrt{\\frac{\\sec \\theta - 1}{\\sec \\theta + 1}}\\)", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "2sin&theta;"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "<span class="mord">2</span><span class="mop">cosec</span><span class="mord mathnormal">&theta;</span>"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "2 tan &theta;"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "<span class="mop">2tan</span><span class="mord mathnormal">&theta;.</span><span class="mop">sec</span><span class="mord mathnormal">&theta;</span>"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "2", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Put θ = 60° in the given expression (since sec 60° = 2).

\n

Expression = √((2 + 1) ÷ (2 − 1)) + √((2 − 1) ÷ (2 + 1))

\n

⇒ √(3/1) + √(1/3) = √3 + 1/√3 = 4/√3

\n

Now, checking the options by substituting θ = 60°:

\n

Only Option 2 gives: 2cosec(60°) = 2 × (2/√3) = 4/√3

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2cosecθ.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

Expression = √[(sec θ + 1) ÷ (sec θ − 1)] + √[(sec θ − 1) ÷ (sec θ + 1)]

\n

Formula Used:

\n

⇒ sec2θ − 1 = tan2θ

\n

⇒ sec θ = 1 / cos θ , tan θ = sin θ / cos θ

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ By taking the LCM of the denominators, we get:

\n

⇒ Expression = [ (sec θ + 1) + (sec θ − 1) ] / √[(sec θ − 1)(sec θ + 1)]

\n

⇒ Expression = (2sec θ) / √(sec2θ − 1)

\n

⇒ Expression = (2sec θ) / √(tan2θ)

\n

⇒ Expression = 2sec θ / tan θ

\n

⇒ Expression = 2(1 / cos θ) ÷ (sin θ / cos θ)

\n

⇒ Expression = 2 / sin θ

\n

⇒ Expression = 2cosec θ

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2cosecθ.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Pythagorean Trigonometric Identities

\n

sin2θ + cos2θ = 1 | sec2θ − tan2θ = 1 | cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1

\n

Reciprocal Identities

\n

sin θ = 1/cosec θ | cos θ = 1/sec θ | tan θ = 1/cot θ

\n

Value Substitution Method

\n

To quickly solve trigonometric simplifications, substitute standard angles like 30°, 45°, or 60°. Always avoid angles that make the expression undefined (e.g., denominators becoming zero).

दिया गया है:

\n\n

व्यंजक है: \\(\\sqrt{\\frac{\\sec \\theta + 1}{\\sec \\theta - 1}} + \\sqrt{\\frac{\\sec \\theta - 1}{\\sec \\theta + 1}}\\)

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

हम दिए गए व्यंजक को सरल करने के लिए बीजगणितीय जोड़-घटाव और त्रिकोणमितीय सर्वसमिकाओं का उपयोग करेंगे।

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

मान लीजिए x = \\(\\sec \\theta\\) है। तब, व्यंजक बन जाता है:

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt{\\frac{x + 1}{x - 1}} + \\sqrt{\\frac{x - 1}{x + 1}}\\)

\n\n

प्रत्येक पद को अलग-अलग सरल कीजिए:

\n\n

\\(\\sqrt{\\frac{x + 1}{x - 1}} = \\frac{\\sqrt{x + 1}}{\\sqrt{x - 1}}, \\quad \\sqrt{\\frac{x - 1}{x + 1}} = \\frac{\\sqrt{x - 1}}{\\sqrt{x + 1}}\\)

\n\n

अब व्यंजक बन जाता है:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{\\sqrt{x + 1}}{\\sqrt{x - 1}} + \\frac{\\sqrt{x - 1}}{\\sqrt{x + 1}}\\)

\n\n

पदों को संयोजित करने के लिए, हमें एक समान हर की आवश्यकता है:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{\\sqrt{x + 1}}{\\sqrt{x - 1}} + \\frac{\\sqrt{x - 1}}{\\sqrt{x + 1}} = \\frac{(\\sqrt{x + 1})^2 + (\\sqrt{x - 1})^2}{\\sqrt{x - 1} \\cdot \\sqrt{x + 1}}\\)

\n\n

अंश को सरल कीजिए:

\n\n

\\((\\sqrt{x + 1})^2 + (\\sqrt{x - 1})^2 = (x + 1) + (x - 1) = 2x\\)

\n\n

इसलिए, व्यंजक बन जाता है:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{2x}{\\sqrt{x^2 - 1}}\\)

\n\n

वापस x = \\(\\sec \\theta\\) प्रतिस्थापित कीजिए:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{2 \\sec \\theta}{\\sqrt{\\sec^2 \\theta - 1}}\\)

\n\n

8. सर्वसमिका \\( \\sec^2 \\theta - 1 = \\tan^2 \\theta\\) का उपयोग करते हुए, व्यंजक बन जाता है:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{2 \\sec \\theta}{\\sqrt{\\tan^2 \\theta}} = \\frac{2 \\sec \\theta}{\\tan \\theta}\\) = 2 \\(cosec \\theta\\)

\n\n

इस प्रकार, सरलीकृत व्यंजक है: 2 \\(cosec \\theta\\)

Shortcut Trick\n

Divide the given equation by cos θ:

\n

tan θ + 1 = √3 ⇒ tan θ = √3 − 1

\n

Since cot θ = 1 / tan θ, we get cot θ = 1 / (√3 − 1)

\n

Multiply numerator and denominator by (√3 + 1):

\n

cot θ = (√3 + 1) / ((√3)2 − 12) = (√3 + 1) / 2

\n

∴ The correct answer is (√3 + 1)/2.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

sin θ + cos θ = √3 cos θ

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ sin θ = √3 cos θ − cos θ

\n

Take cos θ common on the right side:

\n

⇒ sin θ = (√3 − 1) cos θ

\n

We know that cot θ = cos θ / sin θ

\n

⇒ cos θ / sin θ = 1 / (√3 − 1)

\n

⇒ cot θ = 1 / (√3 − 1)

\n

Rationalising the denominator by multiplying and dividing by (√3 + 1):

\n

⇒ cot θ = [1 × (√3 + 1)] / [(√3 − 1) × (√3 + 1)]

\n

Using the algebraic identity (a − b)(a + b) = a2 − b2:

\n

⇒ cot θ = (√3 + 1) / (3 − 1)

\n

⇒ cot θ = (√3 + 1) / 2

\n

∴ The correct answer is (√3 + 1)/2.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1, 1 + tan2 θ = sec2 θ, and 1 + cot2 θ = cosec2 θ.

\n

Trigonometric Ratios

\n

tan θ = sin θ / cos θ and cot θ = cos θ / sin θ. The reciprocal relations are sin θ = 1 / cosec θ, cos θ = 1 / sec θ, and tan θ = 1 / cot θ.

\n

Rationalisation

\n

When the denominator contains a surd, we multiply the numerator and denominator by its conjugate to rationalize it, applying the rule (a + b)(a − b) = a2 − b2.

ಮರುಹೊಂದಿಸಿದಾಗ:

\n\n

sin θ = √3 cos θ - cos θ

\n\n

sin θ = cos θ (√3 - 1).

\n\n

ಹಂತ 2: cot θ ನ ವ್ಯಾಖ್ಯಾನವನ್ನು ಬಳಸಿ:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

ಅಂಶ ಮತ್ತು ಛೇದದಿಂದ cos θ ಅನ್ನು ರದ್ದುಗೊಳಿಸಿ:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

cot θ = (√3 + 1) / (3 - 1).

\n\n

cot θ = (√3 + 1) / 2.

\n\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Apply the Componendo and Dividendo rule directly to the given ratio.

\n

⇒ sec α / tan α = (7 + 4) / (7 − 4)

\n

⇒ (1 / cos α) / (sin α / cos α) = 11 / 3

\n

⇒ 1 / sin α = 11 / 3

\n

⇒ cosec α = 11 / 3

\n

∴ The correct answer is 11/3.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

(sec α + tan α) / (sec α − tan α) = 7/4

\n

Formula Used:

\n

sec α = 1 / cos α

\n

tan α = sin α / cos α

\n

cosec α = 1 / sin α

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ (sec α + tan α) / (sec α − tan α) = 7/4

\n

By cross-multiplying the terms:

\n

⇒ 4 × (sec α + tan α) = 7 × (sec α − tan α)

\n

⇒ 4 sec α + 4 tan α = 7 sec α − 7 tan α

\n

Rearranging the terms to group sec α and tan α:

\n

⇒ 4 tan α + 7 tan α = 7 sec α − 4 sec α

\n

⇒ 11 tan α = 3 sec α

\n

Converting sec α and tan α into sin α and cos α:

\n

⇒ 11 × (sin α / cos α) = 3 × (1 / cos α)

\n

⇒ 11 sin α = 3

\n

⇒ sin α = 3 / 11

\n

Since cosec α = 1 / sin α:

\n

⇒ cosec α = 11 / 3

\n

∴ The correct answer is 11/3.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Componendo and Dividendo Rule

\n

If a fraction a/b = c/d, then (a + b) / (a − b) = (c + d) / (c − d). This rule is highly useful for simplifying rational algebraic and trigonometric equations quickly.

\n

Basic Trigonometric Ratios

\n

sec α is the reciprocal of cos α (sec α = 1/cos α), and cosec α is the reciprocal of sin α (cosec α = 1/sin α).

\n

Pythagorean Identities

\n

The three fundamental identities are: sin2α + cos2α = 1, sec2α − tan2α = 1, and cosec2α − cot2α = 1.

\n\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Divide the entire equation by cos2θ to get: 3tan2θ + 5 = 4sec2θ

\n

Substitute sec2θ = 1 + tan2θ into the equation:

\n

3tan2θ + 5 = 4(1 + tan2θ)

\n

5 − 4 = 4tan2θ − 3tan2θ ⇒ tan2θ = 1

\n

Since θ is an acute angle, tanθ = 1.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

3sin2θ + 5cos2θ = 4

\n

Formula Used:

\n

sin2θ + cos2θ = 1

\n

tanθ = Perpendicular ÷ Base

\n

Calculations:

\n\n

⇒ 3sin2θ + 3cos2θ + 2cos2θ = 4

\n

⇒ 3(sin2θ + cos2θ) + 2cos2θ = 4

\n

⇒ 3(1) + 2cos2θ = 4

\n

⇒ 2cos2θ = 1

\n

⇒ cos2θ = 1 / 2

\n

⇒ cosθ = 1 / √2

\n

⇒ Since θ is an acute angle, θ = 45°

\n

⇒ tanθ = tan(45°) = 1

\n

∴ The correct answer is 1.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

Fundamental identities: sin2θ + cos2θ = 1, sec2θ − tan2θ = 1, and cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1.

\n

Angles in the First Quadrant

\n

For any acute angle (0° < θ < 90°), all six trigonometric ratios (sin, cos, tan, cot, sec, cosec) are positive.

\n

Values at 45°

\n

When θ = 45°, the perpendicular and base of a right triangle are equal in length, giving tan(45°) = 1 and sin(45°) = cos(45°) = 1/√2.

समीकरण है: 3sin²θ + 5cos²θ = 4

\n\n

हमें tanθ का मान ज्ञात करना है, जहाँ θ एक न्यून कोण है।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

हम जानते हैं कि सर्वसमिका: sin²θ + cos²θ = 1

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

सर्वसमिका cos²θ = 1 - sin²θ का उपयोग करके cos²θ को sin²θ के पदों में व्यक्त कीजिए।

\n\n

इसे दिए गए समीकरण में प्रतिस्थापित कीजिए:

\n\n

3sin²θ + 5(1 - sin²θ) = 4

\n\n

समीकरण को सरल कीजिए:

\n\n

3sin²θ + 5 - 5sin²θ = 4

Given:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{{1 + \\sin \\theta }}{{\\cos \\theta }}\\)

\n\n

Concept used:

\n\n

1. a2 - b2 = (a + b)(a - b)

\n\n

2. sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{{1 + \\sin θ }}{{\\cos θ }}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{(1 + \\sin θ)(1 - \\sin θ)}{cos θ \\times (1 - \\sin θ)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{(1 - \\sin^2 θ)}{cos θ \\times (1 - \\sin θ)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{cos^2 θ }{cos θ \\times (1 - \\sin θ)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{{\\cos θ }}{{1 - \\sin θ }}\\)

\n\n

∴ \\(\\frac{{1 + \\sin θ }}{{\\cos θ }}\\) is equal to \\(\\frac{{\\cos θ }}{{1 - \\sin θ }}\\).

\n\n87:T789,\nदिया गया है:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{{1 + \\sin \\theta }}{{\\cos \\theta }}\\)

\n\n

प्रयुक्त अवधारणा:

\n\n

1. a2 - b2 = (a + b)(a - b)

\n\n

2. sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{{1 + \\sin θ }}{{\\cos θ }}\\)

\n\n

⇒ ఇచ్చినది:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{{1 + \\sin \\theta }}{{\\cos \\theta }}\\)

\n\n

ఉపయోగించిన సూత్రం:

\n\n

1. a2 - b2 = (a + b)(a - b)

\n\n

2. sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1

\n\n

గణన:

\n\n

\\(\\frac{{1 + \\sin θ }}{{\\cos θ }}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{(1 + \\sin θ)(1 - \\sin θ)}{cos θ \\times (1 - \\sin θ)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{(1 - \\sin^2 θ)}{cos θ \\times (1 - \\sin θ)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{cos^2 θ }{cos θ \\times (1 - \\sin θ)}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{{\\cos θ }}{{1 - \\sin θ }}\\)

\n\n

∴ \\(\\frac{{1 + \\sin θ }}{{\\cos θ }}\\) అనేది \\(\\frac{{\\cos θ }}{{1 - \\sin θ }}\\) కి సమానం.

Given:

\n\n

sin x = \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\)

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

(1.) sec2θ - tan2θ = 1

\n\n

(2.) cosec2θ - cot2θ = 1

\n\n

(3.) sin(30∘) = \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

According to the question,

\n\n

Since sin(30∘) = \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\),

\n\n

Therefore, x = 30∘

\n\n

Again according to the question,

\n\n

⇒ ? = (sec4x + cosec4x) - (tan4x + cot4x)

\n\n

⇒ ? = sec4x + cosec4x - tan4x - cot4x

\n\n

⇒ ? = sec4x - tan4x + cosec4x - cot4x

\n\n

⇒ ? = (sec2x - tan2x)(sec2x + tan2x) + (cosec2x - cot2x)(cosec2x + cot2x)

\n\n

⇒ ? = (1)(sec2x + tan2x) + (1)(cosec2x + cot2x)

\n\n

⇒ ? = (sec2x + tan2x) + (cosec2x + cot2x)

\n\n

⇒ ? = (sec230∘ + tan230∘) + (cosec230∘ + cot230∘)

\n\n

⇒ ? = (\\(\\frac{2}{√ 3}\\))2 + (\\(\\frac{1}{√ 3}\\))2 + (2)2 + (√3)2

\n\n

⇒ ? = \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\) + \\(\\frac{1}{ 3}\\) + 4 + 3

\n\n

⇒ ? = \\(\\frac{4 + 1 + 12 + 9}{3} = \\frac{26}{3}\\)

\n\n

Therefore, '\\(\\frac{26}{3}\\)' is the required answer.

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ ? = sec4x - tan4x + cosec4x - cot4x

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ ? = (\\(\\frac{2}{√ 3}\\))2 + (\\(\\frac{1}{√ 3}\\))2 + (2)2 + (√3)2

\n\n

⇒ ? = \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\) + \\(\\frac{1}{ 3}\\) + 4 + 3

\n\n

⇒ ? = \\(\\frac{4 + 1 + 12 + 9}{3} = \\frac{26}{3}\\)

\n\n

⇒ ? = (sec4x + cosec4x) - (tan4x + cotGiven:

\n\n

(cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ − sin θ)(cos θ − sec θ) = ________.

\n\n

Concept:

\n\n

We need to simplify the trigonometric expression using trigonometric identities.

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

1. cot θ = 1/tan θ

\n\n

2. sec θ = 1/cos θ

\n\n

3. cosec θ = 1/sin θ

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

We start by simplifying each term individually:

\n\n

⇒ cot θ + tan θ = (cos θ/sin θ) + (sin θ/cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ + sin2 θ) / (sin θ cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = 1 / (sin θ cos θ)            [since cos2 θ + sin2 θ = 1]

\n\n

Next, simplify cosec θ - sin θ:

\n\n

⇒ cosec θ - sin θ = (1/sin θ) - sin θ

\n\n

⇒ = (1 - sin2 θ) / sin θ

\n\n

⇒ = cos2 θ / sin θ            [since 1 - sin2 θ = cos2 θ]

\n\n

Finally, simplify cos θ - sec θ:

\n\n

⇒ cos θ - sec θ = cos θ - (1/cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ - 1) / cos θ

\n\n

⇒ = -sin2 θ / cos θ            [since cos2 θ - 1 = -sin2 θ]

\n\n

Now, combine all three simplified terms:

\n\n

⇒ (cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ - sin θ)(cos θ - sec θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) × (cos2 θ / sin θ) × (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) × (cos2 θ / sin θ) × (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (-1)            [After simplifying the terms and canceling out common factors]

\n\n

∴ The expression equals -1.

दिया गया है:

\n\n

(cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ − sin θ)(cos θ − sec θ) = ________.

\n\n

सिद्धांत:

\n\n

हमें त्रिकोणमितीय सर्वसमिकाओं का उपयोग करके त्रिकोणमितीय व्यंजक को सरल करना होगा।

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n\n

1. cot θ = 1/tan θ

\n\n

2. sec θ = 1/cos θ

\n\n

3. cosec θ = 1/sin θ

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

हम प्रत्येक पद को अलग-अलग सरल करके आरम्भ करते हैं:

\n\n

⇒ cot θ + tan θ = (cos θ/sin θ) + (sin θ/cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ + sin2 θ) / (sin θ cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = 1 / (sin θ cos θ)            [since cos2 θ + sin2 θ = 1]

\n\n

अगला, cosec θ - sin θ को सरल करें:

\n\n

⇒ cosec θ - sin θ = (1/sin θ) - sin θ

\n\n

⇒ = (1 - sin2 θ) / sin θ

\n\n

⇒ = cos2 θ / sin θ         [चूँकि 1 - sin2 θ = cos2 θ]

\n\n

अंत में, cos θ - sec θ को सरल करें:

\n\n

⇒ cos θ - sec θ = cos θ - (1/cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ - 1) / cos θ

\n\n

⇒ = -sin2 θ / cos θ         [चूँकि cos2 θ - 1 = -sin2 θ]

\n\n

अब, तीनों सरलीकृत पदों को मिलाएं:

\n\n

⇒ (cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ - sin θ)(cos θ - sec θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) × (cos2 θ / sin θ) × (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) × (cos2 θ / sin θ) × (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (-1)              [पदों को सरल करने और उभयनिष्ठ गुणनखंडों को रद्द करने के बाद]

\n\n

∴ व्यंजक -1 के बराबर है।

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

दिलेले आहे:

\n\n

(cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ − sin θ)(cos θ − sec θ) = ________.

\n\n

संकल्पना:

\n\n

त्रिकोणमितीय ओळख वापरून आपल्याला त्रिकोणमितीय पदावली सुलभ करणे आवश्यक आहे.

\n\n

वापरलेले सूत्र:

\n\n

1. cot θ = 1/tan θ

\n\n

2. sec θ = 1/cos θ

\n\n

3. cosec θ = 1/sin θ

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

आपण प्रत्येक संज्ञा स्वतंत्रपणे सरलीकृत करून प्रारंभ करू:

\n\n

⇒ cot θ + tan θ = (cos θ/sin θ) + (sin θ/cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ + sin2 θ) / (sin θ cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = 1 / (sin θ cos θ)            [जसे cos2 θ + sin2 θ = 1]

\n\n

पुढे, cosec θ - sin θ सरलीकृत करू:

\n\n

⇒ cosec θ - sin θ = (1/sin θ) - sin θ

\n\n

⇒ = (1 - sin2 θ) / sin θ

\n\n

⇒ = cos2 θ / sin θ            [जसे 1 - sin2 θ = cos2 θ]

\n\n

शेवटी, cos θ - sec θ सरलीकृत करू:

\n\n

⇒ cos θ - sec θ = cos θ - (1/cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ - 1) / cos θ

\n\n

⇒ = -sin2 θ / cos θ            [जसे cos2 θ - 1 = -sin2 θ]

\n\n

आता, सर्व तीन सरलीकृत संज्ञा एकत्र करू:

\n\n

⇒ (cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ - sin θ)(cos θ - sec θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) × (cos2 θ / sin θ) × (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) × (cos2 θ / sin θ) × (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (-1) [अटी सरलीकृत केल्यानंतर आणि सामाईक घटक रद्द केल्यानंतर]

\n\n

∴ पदावली -1 च्या बरोबरीची आहे.

\n\n

\n\n

1. cot θ = 1/tan θ

\n\n

2. sec θ = 1/cos θ

\n\n

3. cosec θ = 1/sin θ

\n\n

গণনা:

\n\n

আমরা প্রথমে প্রতিটি পদকে পৃথকভাবে সরলীকরণ করি:

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ - 1) / cos θ

\n\n

⇒ = -sin2 θ / cos θ [যেহেতু cos2 θ - 1 = -sin2 θ]

\n\n

এখন, তিনটি সরলীকৃত পদকে একত্রিত করি:

\n\n

⇒ (cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ - sin θ)(cos θ - sec θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) x (cos2 θ / sin θ) x (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) x (cos2 θ / sin θ) x (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (-1)         [পদগুলি সরলীকরণ এবং সাধারণ উৎপাদক বাদ দেওয়ার পর]

\n\n

∴ রাশিটি -1 এর সমান

ખ્યાલ:

\n\n

ત્રિકોણમિતિય સમીકરણને ત્રિકોણમિતિય ઓળખનો ઉપયોગ કરીને સરળ બનાવવાની જરૂર છે.

\n\n

વપરાયેલ સૂત્ર:

\n\n

1. cot θ = 1/tan θ

\n\n

2. sec θ = 1/cos θ

\n\n

આપણે દરેક પદને અલગથી સરળ બનાવીને શરૂઆત કરીએ છીએ:

&rAr", "ml": "", "gu": "\n

આપેલ:

\n\n

(cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ − sin θ)(cos θ − sec θ) = ________.

\n\n

ખ્યાલ:

\n\n

ત્રિકોણમિતિય સમીકરણને ત્રિકોણમિતિય ઓળખનો ઉપયોગ કરીને સરળ બનાવવાની જરૂર છે.

\n\n

વપરાયેલ સૂત્ર:

\n\n

1. cot θ = 1/tan θ

\n\n

2. sec θ = 1/cos θ

\n\n

3. cosec θ = 1/sin θ

\n\n

ગણતરી:

\n\n

આપણે દરેક પદને અલગથી સરળ બનાવીને શરૂઆત કરીએ છીએ:

\n\n

⇒ cot θ + tan θ = (cos θ/sin θ) + (sin θ/cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ + sin2 θ) / (sin θ cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = 1 / (sin θ cos θ) [કારણ કે cos2 θ + sin2 θ = 1]

\n\n

આગળ, cosec θ - sin θ ને સરળ બનાવો:

\n\n

⇒ cosec θ - sin θ = (1/sin θ) - sin θ

\n\n

⇒ = (1 - sin2 θ) / sin θ

\n\n

⇒ = cos2 θ / sin θ [કારણ કે 1 - sin2 θ = cos2 θ]

\n\n

છેવટે, cos θ - sec θ ને સરળ બનાવો:

\n\n

⇒ cos θ - sec θ = cos θ - (1/cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ - 1) / cos θ

\n\n

⇒ = -sin2 θ / cos θ [કારણ કે cos2 θ - 1 = -sin2 θ]

\n\n

હવે, ત્રણેય સરળ બનાવેલા પદોને જોડો:

\n\n

⇒ (cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ - sin θ)(cos θ - sec θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) x (cos2 θ / sin θ) x (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) x (cos2 θ / sin θ) x (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (-1) [પદોને સરળ બનાવ્યા પછી અને સામાન્ય પરિબળોને રદ કર્યા પછી]

\n\n

∴ અભિવ્યક્તિ -1 ની બરાબર છે.

\n\n

1. cot θ = 1/tan θ

\n\n

2. sec θ = 1/cos θ

\n\n

3. cosec θ = 1/sin θ

\n\n

ಲೆಕ್ಕಾಚಾರ:

\n\n

ನಾವು ಪ್ರತಿ ಪದವನ್ನು ಪ್ರತ್ಯೇಕವಾಗಿ ಸರಳಗೊಳಿಸುವ ಮೂಲಕ ಪ್ರಾರಂಭಿಸುತ್ತೇವೆ:

\n\n

⇒ cot θ + tan θ = (cos θ/sin θ) + (sin θ/cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ + sin2 θ) / (sin θ cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = 1 / (sin θ cos θ) [ಏಕೆಂದರೆ cos2 θ + sin2 θ = 1]

\n\n

ಮುಂದೆ, cosec θ - sin θ ಅನ್ನು ಸರಳಗೊಳಿಸಿ:

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ = (1 - sin2 θ) / sin θ

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ = (cos2 θ - 1) / cos θ

\n\n

⇒ = -sin2 θ / cos θ [ಏಕೆಂದರೆ cos2 θ - 1 = -sin2 θ]

\n\n

ಈಗ, ಮೂರು ಸರಳಗೊಳಿಸಿದ ಪದಗಳನ್ನು ಸಂಯೋಜಿಸಿ:

\n\n

⇒ (cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ - sin θ)(cos θ - sec θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) x (cos2 θ / sin θ) x (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) x (cos2 θ / sin θ) x (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (-1) [ಪದಗಳನ್ನು ಸರಳಗೊಳಿಸಿದ ನಂತರ ಮತ್ತು ಸಾಮಾನ್ಯ ಅಂಶಗಳನ್ನು ರದ್ದುಗೊಳಿಸಿದ ನಂತರ]

\n\n

∴ ಉಕ್ತಿ -1 ಗೆ ಸಮಾನವಾಗಿರುತ್ತದೆ.

\n\n

\n\n

∴ வெளிப்பாடு -1 க்கு சமம்.

⇒ = -sin2 θ / cos θ [ਕਿਉਂਕਿ cos2 θ - 1 = -sin2 θ]

\n\n

ਹੁਣ, ਸਾਰੇ ਤਿੰਨ ਸਰਲ ਕੀਤੇ ਪਦਾਂ ਨੂੰ ਜੋੜੋ:

\n\n

⇒ (cot θ + tan θ)(cosec θ - sin θ)(cos θ - sec θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) x (cos2 θ / sin θ) x (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (1 / (sin θ cos θ)) x (cos2 θ / sin θ) x (-sin2 θ / cos θ)

\n\n

⇒ = (-1) [ਪਦਾਂ ਨੂੰ ਸਰਲ ਬਣਾਉਣ ਅਤੇ ਸਾਂਝੇ ਕਾਰਕਾਂ ਨੂੰ ਰੱਦ ਕਰਨ ਤੋਂ ਬਾਅਦ]

\n\n

∴ ਸਮੀਕਰਨ -1 ਦੇ ਬਰਾਬਰ ਹੈ।

\n\ne3:T50b,

Given:

\n\n

Expression: (1 - cos2A) sec2A

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

1. secA = 1/cosA

\n\n

2. 1 - cos2A = sin2A

\n\n

3. sinA/cosA = tanA

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

(1 - cos2θ + 3cot2θ = 17, then the value of 'θ' when 0° ≤ θ ≤ 90° is:", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "45&deg;"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "90&deg;"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "30&deg;"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "75&deg;"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}, "hn": {"value": "यदि 2cosec2θ + 3cot2θ = 17 है, तो θ का मान कितना होगा जब 0° ≤ θ ≤ 90° है?", "options": [{"prompt": "1", "value": "45&deg;"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "90&deg;"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "30&deg;"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "75&deg;"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Shortcut Trick\n

Put the values from the options into the equation to find the correct answer.

\n

Check for θ = 30°:

\n

⇒ 2cosec230° + 3cot230° = 2(2)2 + 3(√3)2

\n

⇒ 2 × 4 + 3 × 3 = 8 + 9 = 17.

\n

LHS = RHS, thus 30° is the correct value.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 30°.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

2cosec2θ + 3cot2θ = 17

\n

Formula Used:

\n

cosec2θ = 1 + cot2θ

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ 2(1 + cot2θ) + 3cot2θ = 17

\n

⇒ 2 + 2cot2θ + 3cot2θ = 17

\n

⇒ 5cot2θ = 17 − 2

\n

⇒ 5cot2θ = 15

\n

⇒ cot2θ = 3

\n

⇒ cotθ = √3 (since 0° ≤ θ ≤ 90°)

\n

⇒ θ = 30°

\n

∴ The correct answer is 30°.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identities

\n

1) sin2θ + cos2θ = 1
2) sec2θ − tan2θ = 1
3) cosec2θ − cot2θ = 1

\n

sin 30° = 1/2, cos 30° = √3/2, tan 30° = 1/√3, cosec 30° = 2, sec 30° = 2/√3, cot 30° = √3

selected option prompt let marked = {}; // qid -> true let visited = new Set(); let submitted = false; let timeLeft = DURATION_SEC; let timerInterval = null; // Flatten all questions for easy indexing const allQuestions = []; TEST_DATA.sections.forEach((s, sIdx) => { s.questions.forEach(q => { allQuestions.push({...q, sectionIdx: sIdx, sectionName: s.section_name}); }); }); function init() { // Render section tabs const sectionTabs = document.getElementById('sectionTabs'); TEST_DATA.sections.forEach((s, i) => { const tab = document.createElement('button'); tab.className = 'section-tab' + (i === 0 ? 'high' : 'unidentified'); const confClass = confidence === 'high' ? 'conf-high' : 'conf-unidentified'; headerExtras += '🎯 ' + concept + ''; headerExtras += 'conf: ' + confidence + ''; // Question ID (unique per question, useful for cross-test matching) headerExtras += 'ID: ' + q.qid + ''; document.getElementById('qMarks').innerHTML = headerExtras; // Language tabs const langTabs = document.getElementById('qLangTabs'); langTabs.innerHTML = ''; const availableLangs = Object.keys(q.langs); const showLangs = ['en','hn','te','mr','bn','ml','gu','kn','ta','or'].filter(l => availableLangs.includes(l)); if (showLangs.length > 1) { showLangs.forEach((l, i) => { const btn = document.createElement('button'); btn.className = 'q-lang-tab' + (l === currentLang ? ' active' : ''); btn.textContent = {en:'English',hn:'Hindi',te:'Telugu',mr:'Marathi',bn:'Bengali',ml:'Malayalam',gu:'Gujarati',kn:'Kannada',ta:'Tamil',or:'Odia'}[l] || l; btn.onclick = () => { currentLang = l; renderQuestion(); }; langTabs.appendChild(btn); }); } // Question text const langData = q.langs[currentLang] || q.langs[availableLangs[0]] || {}; document.getElementById('qText').innerHTML = langData.value || '(no question text)'; // Options const optsList = document.getElementById('optionsList'); optsList.innerHTML = ''; (langData.options || []).forEach(opt => { const li = document.createElement('li'); li.className = 'option-item' + (answers[q.qid] === opt.prompt ? } function selectOption(qid, prompt) { if (submitted) return; if (answers[qid] === prompt) { delete answers[qid]; } else { answers[qid] = prompt; } renderQuestion(); } function saveNext() { nextQuestion(); } function markReviewNext() { const q = allQuestions[currentQIdx]; marked[q.qid] = true; nextQuestion(); } function clearResponse() { const q = allQuestions[currentQIdx]; delete answers[q.qid]; renderQuestion(); } function nextQuestion() { if (currentQIdx < allQuestions.length - 1) { currentQIdx++; renderQuestion(); } } function prevQuestion() { if (currentQIdx > 0) { currentQIdx--; renderQuestion(); } } function jumpTo(idx) { currentQIdx = idx; renderQuestion(); window.scrollTo({top: 0, behavior: 'smooth'}); } function switchSection(idx) { currentSectionIdx = idx; // Find first question in this section const qIdx = allQuestions.findIndex(q => q.sectionIdx === idx); if (qIdx >= 0) { currentQIdx = qIdx; renderQuestion(); } } function renderPalette() { const grid = document.getElementById('paletteGrid'); grid.innerHTML = ''; allQuestions.forEach((q, i) => { const cell = document.createElement('div'); let cls = 'palette-cell '; const isAnswered = answers[q.qid] !== undefined; const isMarked = marked[q.qid]; const isVisited = visited.has(q.qid); if (isMarked && isAnswered) cls += 'marked-answered'; else if (isMarked) cls += 'marked'; else if (isAnswered) cls += 'answered'; else if (isVisited) cls += 'notanswered'; else cls += 'notvisited'; if (i === currentQIdx) cls += ' current'; cell.className = cls; cell.textContent = (i + 1); cell.onclick = () => jumpTo(i); grid.appendChild(cell); }); } function updateStats() { let answered = 0, notAnswered = 0, marked = 0, notVisited = 0; allQuestions.forEach((q, i) => { const isAnswered = answers[q.qid] !== undefined; const isMarked = marked[q.qid]; const isVisited = visited.has(q.qid); if (isMarked && isAnswered) marked++; else if (isMarked) marked++; else if (isAnswered) answered++; else if (isVisited) notAnswered++; else notVisited++; }); document.getElementById('paletteStats').innerHTML = `

Answered:${answered}
Not Answered:${notAnswered}
Marked:${marked}
Not Visited:${notVisited}
`; } function openSubmitModal() { let answered = 0, marked = 0, notVisited = 0; allQuestions.forEach((q, i) => { if (answers[q.qid] !== undefined) answered++; else if (marked[q.qid]) marked++; else if (!visited.has(q.qid)) notVisited++; }); document.getElementById('submitSummary').innerHTML = `

Not Answered:${TOTAL_Q - answered - notVisited}
Not Visited:${notVisited}
Total:${TOTAL_Q}

${score.toFixed(2)} / ${TEST_DATA.max_marks || TOTAL_Q * 2}
Correct: ${correct} | Wrong: ${wrong} | Skipped: ${skipped}
Accuracy: ${(correct + wrong > 0) ? ((correct / (correct + wrong)) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0}%
`; // Render all questions with answers + solutions renderAllQuestions(); } function renderAllQuestions() { const container = document.getElementById('resultsQuestions'); container.innerHTML = ''; allQuestions.forEach((q, i) => { const userAns = answers[q.qid]; const correctAns = q.correct_option; const isCorrect = userAns === correctAns; const isWrong = userAns !== undefined && userAns !== correctAns; const isSkipped = userAns === undefined; const card = document.createElement('div'); card.className = 'result-card ' + (isCorrect ?   • ${opt.prompt}${opt.value}
    • `; }); const sol = q.solution_html[currentLang] || q.solution_html['en'] || ''; const solHtml = sol ? `
    📖 Solution
    ${sol}
    ` : ''; let statusBadge = ''; if (isCorrect) statusBadge = '✓ Correct'; else if (isWrong) statusBadge = '✗ Wrong'; else statusBadge = '— Skipped'; // Concept + confidence + question ID (same as AI JSON) const concept = q.concept || (q.tags && q.tags.length ? q.tags.join(', ') : 'Unidentified'); const confidence = q.confidence || (q.tags && q.tags.length ? 'high' : 'unidentified'); const confClass = confidence === 'high' ? 'conf-high' : 'conf-unidentified'; card.innerHTML = `

    ${langData.value || '(no text)'}
      ${optionsHtml}
    ${solHtml} `; container.appendChild(card); }); } function showAllQuestions() { document.getElementById('resultsView').scrollIntoView({behavior: 'smooth'}); } // Keyboard navigation document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (submitted) return; if (e.target.tagName === 'INPUT' || e.target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA') return; if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'j') nextQuestion(); if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'k') prevQuestion(); if (e.key === '1' || e.key === 'a') { const q = allQuestions[currentQIdx]; const opts = (q.langs[currentLang]||{}).options||[]; if(opts[0]) selectOption(q.qid, opts[0].prompt); } if (e.key === '2' || e.key === 'b') { const q = allQuestions[currentQIdx]; const opts = (q.langs[currentLang]||{}).options||[]; if(opts[1]) selectOption(q.qid, opts[1].prompt); } if (e.key === '3' || e.key === 'c') { const q = allQuestions[currentQIdx]; const opts = (q.langs[currentLang]||{}).options||[]; if(opts[2]) selectOption(q.qid, opts[2].prompt); } if (e.key === '4' || e.key === 'd') { const q = allQuestions[currentQIdx]; const opts = (q.langs[currentLang]||{}).options||[]; if(opts[3]) selectOption(q.qid, opts[3].prompt); } }); init();
